# Blind vs every compared receiver: full data (regenerable)

Generated by `BLIND_IMPLEMENTATION/testing/comparison_data.py` from existing results only (est4 `est4/results/bler_{e4,e5,ars}-*.json` via `plot_e4.e4`, blind via `plot_e4.blind`); rerun it when the est5 parts land. SNR axis: SNR (Es/N0) [dB] = burst energy per data symbol (pilot energy charged) unless marked raw. No point below -5 dB is used. 0-error points never enter an interpolation (plot_e4.gap_db). Counts: errors / packets; BLER with Clopper-Pearson 95 %; 0 errors = '< 3/n (95 %)'.

R = 1/10 is the reference rate; main rates 0.15, 2/5, 1/2. 1/10 and 0.15 are separate sections everywhere.

Status of the est5 full runs: e5-10 ok, e5-15 ok, e5-25 ok, e5-2 ok, e5-pp10 ok, e5-pp15 ok, e5-pp25 ok, e5-pp2 ok

## A. Receiver cards

Nk = known symbols sent in addition to the 1024 data symbols; pilot energy = 10 log10((1024 + Nk) / 1024) dB (charged on the SNR axis); airtime overhead = Nk / 1024 (08 8.3 quotes Nk / (1024 + Nk): 10.2 % for 116, 11.1 % for 128). window / lobes / n_hyp = the run_bler settings found in the part files (window = CRC-search half-width M in steps from the receiver's own CRB at that SNR, so it varies with SNR; lobes = lobe hypotheses each side; n_hyp = hypotheses per packet), as min-max over all points of the full and smoke parts.
Our CFO range for every receiver: uniform |f| <= 0.03 cycles/symbol, static over the burst.

### Blind

- Real system / standard: ours: no preamble, no pilots (blind CFO + phase from the polar decoder metric)
- Link: BLIND_IMPLEMENTATION/08_parameters_results.md 8.1; testing/run.py
- Known-symbol layout: none (1024 data symbols only)
- Nk = 0; pilot energy 0.00 dB; airtime +0.0 %
- Estimator chain: testing receiver: run.py testing_s11g at 1/10, 0.15 (nph + q_fix + spec11 below 1/10 -3.5, 0.15 -2.5 dB); 2/5, 1/2: base25_qfix / base2_qfix where run, the presentation receiver (blind_ber.json) elsewhere
- CRC / lobe search: grid of CFO cells x phases ranked by the list-1 decoder metric, CA-SCL-8 + CRC-16 + RHO_N re-encoding check
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: designed for |f| <= 0.03 cycles/symbol (= our range)
- What we changed vs the real system: -
- Data: agg_cmp (blind parts); label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system: none: ours (no preamble, no pilots); spectral efficiency vs the real systems in section E

### Genie

- Real system / standard: none (lower bound): true CFO and true per-symbol phase
- Link: -
- Known-symbol layout: none (1024 data symbols only)
- Nk = 0; pilot energy 0.00 dB; airtime +0.0 %
- Estimator chain: true f and phase, gain 1
- CRC / lobe search: one decode, no search (part files: window 0, lobes 0, n_hyp 0)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: n/a
- What we changed vs the real system: -
- Data: e4-10, e4-15, e4-2, e4-25, e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b, e5-smoke-c; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system: none: lower bound; spectral efficiency vs the real systems in section E

### RCS2-13

- Real system / standard: DVB-RCS2 return-link burst, ID 13 layout (ETSI EN 301 545-2, current V1.5.1 2026-04; est4 built on V1.3.1, ID 13 unchanged; REFERENCES C1, C2, C4)
- Link: https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `rcs13`, K = 1140: [k 32] ([d 19] [k 1]) x 53 [d 17] [k 31]
- Nk = 116; pilot energy 0.47 dB; airtime +11.3 %
- Estimator chain: Rife-Boorstyn FFT over all known symbols (zeros at the data) + 3-bin parabolic interpolation; constant phase
- CRC / lobe search: metric-ordered CRC search over the CRB window; no alias lobes (pilot spacing 20 -> resolves |f| < 0.025) (part files: window 2-3, lobes 0, n_hyp 4-6)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: TR 101 545-4: 0.5-3 % of Rs = 0.005-0.03 cycles/symbol; our 0.03 is the top of that range
- What we changed vs the real system: ID 13 scaled to 1024 data symbols (pre 32, 53 x (19 d + 1 p), 17 d, post 31); turbo -> our polar; constant phase (no phase noise here)
- Data: ars-10, ars-15, ars-2, ars-25, e4-10, e4-15, e4-2, e4-25; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ETSI EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1 (2026-04, current; NGSO, roll-off 0.20 / 0.10 / 0.05, S2X waveforms on the return link) [std]; Annex A ID 13 unchanged from V1.3.1 (used in est4) [std]; guidelines TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1 (2026) [std]
  - Real deployments: MF-TDMA return link (terminal -> hub); DVB-RCS gen 1: > 370 networks, > 180 000 terminals (2010) [vendor]; RCS2 in STM SatLink (2012), Gilat SkyEdge, Creonic hub IP [vendor]; a current RCS2 terminal count: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: ID 13: 1616 symbols, QPSK 1/3, pre 32 + post 31 + 77 one-symbol pilots (P = 20) = 140 known (8.7 %, 0.39 dB) [std, derived]; ours: same pre / post / P scaled to 1024 data, 116 known (0.47 dB): equivalent
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: no TC-LM estimator in the standard or TR (TR: frequency from the preamble) [std]; the one documented implementation (Griebel 2021): DA-KS R-B FFT over all known symbols + 3-bin interpolation, constant phase, one decode [paper]; one shot per burst, the NCC closes a slow per-terminal frequency loop (10 Hz steps) [std]; no phase tracking in any RCS2 source
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: TR 101 545-4: 0.005-0.03 (0.5-3 % of Rs) [std]; Griebel simulates |f| <= 0.015 [paper]; ours 0.03 = the top of the range
  - Published sync loss: Griebel best DA-KS 0.12-0.15 dB vs genie at FER 1e-3 [paper]; TR bench, all impairments: ~0.5 dB (QPSK 1/3), ~0.6 dB (QPSK 1/2) at PER 1e-5 [std]; ours ~0 dB except 1/10 (0.63 dB, 1/20 alias)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, ideal-sync AWGN, PER 1e-3 (TR 101 545-4 10.2.1) [std], net SE [derived]: ID 42 BPSK 1/3 (3236 sym) -3.81 dB 0.304; ID 43 BPSK 1/2 -1.53 dB 0.465; ID 13 QPSK 1/3 (1616) -0.80 dB 0.609; ID 3 QPSK 1/3 (536) -0.27 dB 0.567; ID 2 (262) 0.50 dB 0.427; ID 14 QPSK 1/2 (1616) 1.49 dB 0.931; ID 4 QPSK 1/2 (536) 1.92 dB 0.881; at the same Es/N0 0.56-1.13x our SE (ID 42 0.95x with the bench loss)
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the real system because it gets a metric-ordered CRC search, an 8192-point FFT, the exact LS phase and genie timing; the deployed chain would be 0.1-0.5 dB worse
  - Sources: [EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf), [TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/101500_101599/10154504/01.02.01_60/tr_10154504v010201p.pdf), [Griebel 2021](https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/); lit_card_sat.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.3

### RCS2-13 track

- Real system / standard: as RCS2-13 + pilot phase tracking (ESA US7409024 PA-SWI idea, REFERENCES C9; ours: no DVB-RCS2 source tracks phase in TC-LM)
- Link: as RCS2-13
- Known-symbol layout: `rcs13`, K = 1140: [k 32] ([d 19] [k 1]) x 53 [d 17] [k 31]
- Nk = 116; pilot energy 0.47 dB; airtime +11.3 %
- Estimator chain: as RCS2-13 + per-pilot phase, smoothed (RTS / sliding window), linearly interpolated across the data
- CRC / lobe search: as RCS2-13 (part files: window 2-3, lobes 0, n_hyp 4-6)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: as RCS2-13
- What we changed vs the real system: as RCS2-13; in AWGN (pn 0) its packets are identical to RCS2-13 (same errors): tracking only matters under phase noise
- Data: e4-10, e4-15, e4-2, e4-25; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: **NO: plot_e4 never draws it**
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ETSI EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1 (2026-04, current; NGSO, roll-off 0.20 / 0.10 / 0.05, S2X waveforms on the return link) [std]; Annex A ID 13 unchanged from V1.3.1 (used in est4) [std]; guidelines TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1 (2026) [std]
  - Real deployments: MF-TDMA return link (terminal -> hub); DVB-RCS gen 1: > 370 networks, > 180 000 terminals (2010) [vendor]; RCS2 in STM SatLink (2012), Gilat SkyEdge, Creonic hub IP [vendor]; a current RCS2 terminal count: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: ID 13: 1616 symbols, QPSK 1/3, pre 32 + post 31 + 77 one-symbol pilots (P = 20) = 140 known (8.7 %, 0.39 dB) [std, derived]; ours: same pre / post / P scaled to 1024 data, 116 known (0.47 dB): equivalent
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: no TC-LM estimator in the standard or TR (TR: frequency from the preamble) [std]; the one documented implementation (Griebel 2021): DA-KS R-B FFT over all known symbols + 3-bin interpolation, constant phase, one decode [paper]; one shot per burst, the NCC closes a slow per-terminal frequency loop (10 Hz steps) [std]; no phase tracking in any RCS2 source
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: TR 101 545-4: 0.005-0.03 (0.5-3 % of Rs) [std]; Griebel simulates |f| <= 0.015 [paper]; ours 0.03 = the top of the range
  - Published sync loss: Griebel best DA-KS 0.12-0.15 dB vs genie at FER 1e-3 [paper]; TR bench, all impairments: ~0.5 dB (QPSK 1/3), ~0.6 dB (QPSK 1/2) at PER 1e-5 [std]; ours ~0 dB except 1/10 (0.63 dB, 1/20 alias)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, ideal-sync AWGN, PER 1e-3 (TR 101 545-4 10.2.1) [std], net SE [derived]: ID 42 BPSK 1/3 (3236 sym) -3.81 dB 0.304; ID 43 BPSK 1/2 -1.53 dB 0.465; ID 13 QPSK 1/3 (1616) -0.80 dB 0.609; ID 3 QPSK 1/3 (536) -0.27 dB 0.567; ID 2 (262) 0.50 dB 0.427; ID 14 QPSK 1/2 (1616) 1.49 dB 0.931; ID 4 QPSK 1/2 (536) 1.92 dB 0.881; at the same Es/N0 0.56-1.13x our SE (ID 42 0.95x with the bench loss)
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the real system: as RCS2-13, plus a Kalman / RTS phase tracker that no DVB-RCS2 source has
  - Sources: [EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf), [TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/101500_101599/10154504/01.02.01_60/tr_10154504v010201p.pdf), [Griebel 2021](https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/); lit_card_sat.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.3

### RCS2 pre/post

- Real system / standard: DVB-RCS2 16 + 16 pre / postamble burst, NDA 4th-power (TR 101 545-4; Griebel 2021 NDA chain)
- Link: https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/
- Known-symbol layout: `prepost16`, K = 1056: [k 16] [d 1024] [k 16]
- Nk = 32; pilot energy 0.13 dB; airtime +3.1 %
- Estimator chain: 4th-power FFT over the whole burst (NDA), refined on the 32 known symbols; pi/2 ambiguity from the known symbols
- CRC / lobe search: metric-ordered CRC search (+-3 lobes of 1/1040) (part files: window 2-5, lobes 3, n_hyp 34-76)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: 4th power unambiguous over +-1/8
- What we changed vs the real system: 16 + 16 chosen as the shortest DVB-RCS2-like pre/post; our polar code
- Data: e4-pp10, e4-pp15, e4-pp2, e4-pp25, e5-pp10, e5-pp15, e5-pp2, e5-pp25; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: as DVB-RCS2 (EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1) [std]
  - Real deployments: as DVB-RCS2
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: the pilotless RCS2 format: ID 15, 1616 symbols, QPSK 2/3, pre / post 16 / 16, no pilots (2.0 % known) [std]; ours 16 + 16 around 1024 data (0.13 dB): equivalent
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: Griebel 2021: NDA |r| e^{j4 arg r} FFT for pilotless bursts, QPSK NDA used only at Es/N0 >= 3.9 dB [paper]; one shot, no decoder step
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: as DVB-RCS2 (0.005-0.03) [std]
  - Published sync loss: not published for the NDA chain
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): the pilotless QPSK 2/3 IDs 5 / 15 need 3.90 / 3.46 dB (above our range) [std]; every RCS2 waveform in -5 ... +3 dB carries pilots (IDs 2-4, 13, 14, 42, 43: see RCS2-13)
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: its CRC lobe search is stronger than the real NDA chain, but we run a pilotless 4th-power layout at -5 ... +3 dB, which RCS2 uses only above 3.4 dB
  - Sources: lit_card_sat.md 1.2-1.3, lit_rates.md 4.3

### VDE-SAT

- Real system / standard: C6 VDE-SAT (VHF Data Exchange via satellite, ITU-R M.2092-2 Annex 5 A5-2.10)
- Link: https://www.itu.int/dms_pubrec/itu-r/rec/m/R-REC-M.2092-2-202602-I!!PDF-E.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `vde27`, K = 1090: [k 27] [d 13] ([k 1] [d 26]) x 38 [k 1] [d 23]
- Nk = 66; pilot energy 0.27 dB; airtime +6.4 %
- Estimator chain: ML on the 27-symbol syncword, Rife-Boorstyn over all known symbols in its lobe
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over the +-1/27 alias lobes (alias peak ~0.59 of the true one) (part files: window 2-5, lobes 1, n_hyp 14-32)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: real: LEO Doppler up to ~+-4 kHz at 33.6 ksps = +-0.12 cycles/symbol (IEEE 8780774 estimator); ours +-0.03
- What we changed vs the real system: one 1090-symbol window of an 80371-symbol burst; turbo -> polar; no link ID (none on the downlink, M.2092-2 Table 10); pilot comb as A5-2.10 (fixed 2026-11-15, lit_vdesat_format.md)
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b, e5-smoke-c; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ITU-R M.2092-2 (02/2026) Annex 2 Tables 9-11 + Annex 5 [std]; IALA G1139 ed. 3 (2019) cross-check [std]
  - Real deployments: NorSat-2 (2017; ESA downlink verification, Kongsberg Seatex receivers, 103 passes) [agency]; Andreotti 2021/23 uplink Link ID 20 trial [paper]; SATMAR (Alen Space, 2025) [vendor]; still in trials
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: SAT-MCS-1.50-1 (Link ID 26): 27-symbol syncword every 2268 + 1 pilot / 27 (first +14), no link ID on the downlink, ramp 14 + 14, 80 371-symbol burst, 4.8 % known (0.21 dB) [std, derived]; ours: the same pattern in a 1090-symbol window, 66 known (0.27 dB): faithful, 0.06 dB more energy
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: Ma 2018: correlator bank of Doppler-shifted known sequences, then an autocorrelation-argument fine stage, +-4 kHz [paper, abstract]; Andreotti: DA 'such as R-B' on preamble / pilots + pilot phase [snippet]; tracked over 35 syncwords / 2940 pilots per 2.4 s burst
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: LEO Doppler +-4 kHz + 1 ppm (160 Hz) at 33.6 ksym/s = up to +-0.124 (4.1x ours), drifting ~50 Hz/s [std, derived]; ours +-0.03 static
  - Published sync loss: not found (Ma 2018: 'approximate to the CRB' only; NorSat-2 page gives no numbers)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, AWGN, PER not stated (M.2092-2 T10-11) [std], net SE [derived]: SAT-MCS-0.50-2 (ID 32) BPSK/CDMA 1/4 -4.5 dB 0.19 (0.88x ours); 0.50-3 (ID 33) BPSK 1/3 -3.6 dB 0.325 (1.14x); 1.50-1 (ID 26, the C6 layout) pi/4-QPSK 1/4 -2.4 dB 0.475 (1.11x); 1.50-2 (ID 34) pi/4-QPSK 1/3 -0.6 dB 0.633 (0.99x); the 27 / 27 comb is designed for -2.4 and -0.6 dB only, -4.5 dB is BPSK/CDMA SF 8 with 48-symbol syncwords and a pilot every 8 (22.7 % known)
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: faithful layout and estimator family, stronger by the smaller CFO range and the CRC lobe search, weaker by one syncword (vs tracking 35) and 0.06 dB more energy; at 1/10 and 0.15 it runs 1-2.6 dB below the layout's -2.4 dB design point
  - Sources: [M.2092-2](https://www.itu.int/dms_pubrec/itu-r/rec/m/R-REC-M.2092-2-202602-I!!PDF-E.pdf), [Ma 2018](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/8780774/); lit_card_space.md 5, lit_rates.md 4.5

### DVB-S2 style

- Real system / standard: C7 DVB-S2-inspired (EN 302 307 / TR 102 376-1: PL header + pilot block)
- Link: https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/102300_102399/10237601/01.02.01_60/tr_10237601v010201p.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `dvbs2`, K = 1150: [k 90] [d 512] [k 36] [d 512]
- Nk = 126; pilot energy 0.50 dB; airtime +12.3 %
- Estimator chain: M&M on the 90-symbol PL header, then joint ML header + 36-symbol pilot block
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over +-3 lobes of 1/575 (~30 % of packets start in the wrong lobe at -5 dB) (part files: window 2-5, lobes 3, n_hyp 34-76)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: real: pilot FLL coarse acquisition over many frames (Ku DTH +-5 MHz at 25 Mbaud = 0.2) then fine; one-burst header acquisition range smaller than +-0.03 (lit_real_space.md)
- What we changed vs the real system: real: a pilot block every 16 slots (first one 1440 data symbols after the header: none in a 1024-symbol window), pi/2-BPSK header (only the 26-symbol SOF known before PLS decoding in ACM), multi-frame loops; we give one burst, one block and all 90 header symbols as known
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ETSI EN 302 307-1 V1.4.1 (DVB-S2) and EN 302 307-2 V1.4.1 (2024; V1.3.1 read, DVB-S2X) [std]; TR 102 376-1 / -2 [std]
  - Real deployments: forward / broadcast continuous TDM (DTH, VSAT outbound); > 1.5 billion DVB receivers (all DVB, 2019) [std body]; S2X modems Newtec, Hughes, iDirect, Creonic [vendor]; bursts only as S2X superframe / beam hopping and S2X-in-TDMA on the RCS2 V1.5.1 return link [std]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: PLHEADER 90 (SOF 26 + PLS 64, pi/2-BPSK) + optional 36-symbol pilot block every 16 slots (period 1476); short QPSK frame 8370 symbols, 270 known (3.2 %) [std, derived]; a 1024-symbol window after the header holds NO pilot block, and in ACM / VCM only the 26-symbol SOF is known before PLS decoding; ours: 90 + one 36-block (126, 0.50 dB, period 602)
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: multi-frame: pilot FLL coarse + Luise-Reggiannini fine over ~1000 pilot blocks, per-block ML phase + interpolation (TR 102 376-1) [std]; MathWorks VL-SNR chain: FLL 20 % -> ~2 %, pilot fine 3.5 %, 9-25 acquisition frames [vendor]; no decoder-aided step (research only)
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: acquisition 0.2 (Ku LNB +-5 MHz at 25 Mbaud) over many frames; single frame / tracking 3.4e-4 ... 0.01 (VL-SNR detector designed for <= 1 % of Rs) [std]; ours 0.03 in one burst
  - Published sync loss: sync loss QPSK 1/2 ~0.1 dB, 8PSK 2/3 ~0.25 dB; L&R RMS 6e-5 at -2 dB with 1000 blocks [std]
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): LDPC, ideal-sync AWGN [std], net SE with pilots [derived]: S2 (PER 1e-7, 64 800): QPSK 1/4 -2.35 dB 0.479, 1/3 -1.24 0.641, 2/5 -0.30 0.771, 1/2 1.00 0.965, 3/5 2.23 1.160; S2X (FER 1e-5): BPSK 11/45 -5.50 0.230, BPSK 4/15 short -4.9 <= 0.244, BPSK 1/3 -4.00 0.317, QPSK 2/9 -2.85 0.425, 11/45 short -2.50 0.444, 13/45 -2.03 0.554, 14/45 short -1.46 0.573, 9/20 0.22 0.868, 7/15 short 0.60 0.874, 8/15 short 1.45 1.003, 11/20 1.45 1.063: 1.06-1.32x our SE, mostly from 16k-65k-bit blocks
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than a real 1024-symbol DVB-S2 segment because it adds a pilot block the window does not contain, treats all 90 header symbols as known (CCM) and has genie timing (the real multi-frame tracking chain does not apply to one burst)
  - Sources: [EN 302 307-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/302300_302399/30230701/01.04.01_60/en_30230701v010401p.pdf), [EN 302 307-2](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/302300_302399/30230702/01.03.01_60/en_30230702v010301p.pdf); lit_card_sat.md 2, lit_rates.md 4.1-4.2

### CCSDS style

- Real system / standard: C8 CCSDS-inspired (CCSDS 131.2 SCCC: frame marker + 16-symbol pilot fields; 130.11-G-2)
- Link: https://public.ccsds.org/Pubs/130x11g2.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `ccsds8`, K = 1152: ([k 16] [d 146]) x 2 [k 16] [d 147] [k 16] [d 146] [k 16] [d 147] ([k 16] [d 146]) x 2 [k 16]
- Nk = 128; pilot energy 0.51 dB; airtime +12.5 %
- Estimator chain: Rife-Boorstyn FFT over the 8 x 16 pilot blocks
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over +-5 lobes of 1/162.3 (block period 162.3, data gaps 146 / 147: dense aliases) (part files: window 2-4, lobes 5, n_hyp 54-98)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: real: frame-level FLL over 133 760-symbol frames; 130.11 test case 0.01-0.02 (100 MBaud), ~0.2 at 5 Mbaud (Bertolucci 2021), acquired over many frames, not in one burst; ours +-0.03
- What we changed vs the real system: field spacing scaled to our burst (8 blocks, gaps 146 / 147): 3.7x the real 131.2 pilot density (16 per 540 data); +-5 lobe search added
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: CCSDS 131.2-B-2 (2023, SCCC) + 130.11-G-2 (2023, reference receiver) [std]; 131.0-B-5 (2023, ASM-only TM coding) [std]
  - Real deployments: high-rate EO downlink; HW validation Tesat EM transmitter + Kongsberg Spacetec receiver at 500 MBaud [std]; ground receivers Safran Cortex HDR (1000+ units), Kongsberg HRDFEP [vendor]; named 131.2 flight missions: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: 131.2: 320-symbol header (256 FM + 64 FD) + 16 x 15 x (540 data + 16 pilots), 133 760 symbols, 3.06 % known (0.14 dB) [std, derived]: a 1024-data window holds ~30 known, aliases 1/556; ours 8 x 16 = 128 (0.51 dB), block period 162.3 (aliases 0.0062): 3.7x the real pilot density
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: frame-level 2nd-order FLL (1024-point FFT of the FM + Newton, k1 = 0.2), DA ML phase per 16-symbol field + linear interpolation, no DD or code-aided step; acquisition not simulated [std]; Bertolucci 2021: M&M + Fitz Type II FLL [paper]
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: 130.11 test case 0.01-0.02 (1 MHz + 1 MHz Doppler at 100 MBaud) [std]; ~0.2 at 5 Mbaud, 0.002 at 500 Mbaud (Bertolucci) [paper]; acquired by a multi-frame FLL, never in one burst
  - Published sync loss: sync chain 0.2-0.4 dB at CER 1e-4 (0.2-0.8 dB with a TWTA) [std]; vendor < 0.5 dB (Cortex CRT; HRDFEP snippet) [vendor]
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): SCCC lowest ACM format (QPSK) -0.58 dB at CER 1e-4 [std]; 131.0 turbo k 8920, BER 1e-6 ~ FER 1e-4 [std, derived Es/N0]: 1/6 -4.87 dB 0.332 (1.78x ours), 1/4 -2.86 0.498 (1.34x), 1/3 -1.36 0.664 (1.21x), 1/2 1.10 0.996 (1.15x); AR4JA LDPC (2048, 1024) r 1/2 = our N, K: 2.20 dB at BLER 1e-5, 0.985 (1.00x) [vendor]
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: STRONGER in estimation (3.7x the pilot density, 3.4x wider alias spacing, CRC lobe search), WEAKER in energy (0.51 vs ~0.13 dB charged); the real receiver's CFO is already tracked by its multi-frame FLL
  - Sources: [131.2-B-2](https://public.ccsds.org/Pubs/131x2b2.pdf), [130.11-G-2](https://public.ccsds.org/Pubs/130x11g2.pdf), [130.1-G-3](https://public.ccsds.org/Pubs/130x1g3e1.pdf); lit_card_space.md 2-4, lit_rates.md 4.4

### ViaSat UW

- Real system / standard: C9 ViaSat return-link burst demodulators (a merge of two patents: US 7653150 burst + single peak, US 8243859 top-N + UW joint metric)
- Link: https://patents.google.com/patent/US7653150B2/en
- Known-symbol layout: `viasat16`, K = 1040: [d 512] [k 16] [d 512]
- Nk = 16; pilot energy 0.07 dB; airtime +1.6 %
- Estimator chain: 4th-power FFT over the burst, top-32 peaks (US 8243859) each tested on the 16-symbol midamble UW (pi/2 ambiguity); phase = LS gain on the 16 UW symbols (the patent: 4th-power bins / 4)
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search; window from the 4th-power bound over the burst (crb_nda4) (part files: window 2-16, lobes 0, n_hyp 4-32)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: patent design statement: up to 10 % of Rs (0.1 cycles/symbol; no product spec found); ours +-0.03
- What we changed vs the real system: UW pick = |U|^2 / (Nk s2) + |X4|^2 / mean in-range |X4|^2 (weight 1, from the patent's joint metric); the 16-symbol UW barely ranks the 32 candidates (review 2026-11-15)
- Data: e5-pp10, e5-pp15, e5-pp2, e5-pp25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: patents US 7653150 B2 (2010, expired 2022; continuation of US 7200185) and US 8243859 B2 (2012, active) [patent]; no standard
  - Real deployments: GEO return link of a star MF-TDMA VSAT system (7653150); gateway or mesh terminal (8243859) [patent]; Viasat bought ECC in 2005 (IPSTAR modem chips) [vendor]; product running these algorithms: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: 7653150: 512 QPSK symbols, no preamble, 16-symbol midamble UW inside the coded burst (26 dedicated TPC bits, 3.1 % known) [patent, my reading]; 8243859: UW length not stated; ours: 16 extra symbols (0.07 dB): ~equal cost
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: 7653150: phase x 4 LUT, 1024-point FFT, single peak + 3-bin average, frequency AND phase from the 4th-power bins / 4, UW only for the pi/2 ambiguity and +-8 symbol timing; 8243859: top-N peaks (32 of 1024, N adapted to SNR) x UW positions, joint metric [patent]; one shot, no decoder step
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: 7653150: up to 0.1 (10 % of Rs; a patent design statement, no product spec) [patent]; 4th-power range +-1/8 [derived]; ours 0.03
  - Published sync loss: ~0.2 dB sync degradation; sync fails just below the FEC threshold [patent]; 8243859: burst error < 1e-5 at 2.5 dB, N = 32 [patent]
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): TPC (32,26)^2 rate 0.66, BER 1e-5 at Eb/N0 1.4 dB = Es/N0 ~2.6 dB [patent, derived]; no public ViaSat burst-modem threshold table (LinkWay S2 datasheet 404); its return links use DVB-RCS / RCS2 waveforms (see RCS2-13)
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: it merges the two patents and adds a CRC search and genie timing (stronger), but takes its phase from the 16 UW symbols instead of the 4th-power bins (std 0.31 / 0.18 / 0.14 rad at -5 / 0 / +2 dB), so it is WEAKER at 2/5 and 1/2, the only rates where it works
  - Sources: [US 7653150](https://patents.google.com/patent/US7653150B2/en), [US 8243859](https://patents.google.com/patent/US8243859); lit_card_sat.md 3, lit_rates.md 4.9

### DMRS 1/7

- Real system / standard: C10 LTE / NB-IoT uplink DMRS style (NPUSCH: 1 reference symbol per 7)
- Link: https://arxiv.org/pdf/1606.04171
- Known-symbol layout: `dmrs7`, K = 1195: ([d 3] [k 1] [d 3]) x 170 [d 3] [k 1] [d 1]
- Nk = 171; pilot energy 0.67 dB; airtime +16.7 %
- Estimator chain: Rife-Boorstyn over the pilots (range +-0.071, no alias inside +-0.03)
- CRC / lobe search: metric-ordered CRC search, no lobes (part files: window 2-4, lobes 0, n_hyp 4-8)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: pilot comb unambiguous over +-0.071 (> our +-0.03)
- What we changed vs the real system: single-tone NPUSCH format 1 is structurally the same comb (1 DMRS per 7 single-carrier symbols); multi-tone NPUSCH / LTE PUSCH DMRS blocks are not modelled; window +-0.071 (deployed +-0.018)
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: 3GPP TS 36.211 cl. 10.1 (NPUSCH, DMRS 10.1.4), Rel-13 on; IoT-NTN Rel-17 / 18 [std]
  - Real deployments: 32 NB-IoT networks by early 2018 (GSMA) [vendor]; Qualcomm FSM small-cell eNB (Chakrapani 2019) [paper]; NTN: Skylo GEO Rel-17 (Soracom launch 07/2024), Sateliot LEO, Sony ALT1250 / u-blox / Quectel / Telit modules [vendor]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: single-tone NPUSCH format 1: 1 DMRS in 7 SC-FDMA symbols (symbol 3 at 15 kHz, 4 at 3.75 kHz), pi/2-BPSK / pi/4-QPSK [std, paper]: our 1/7 comb (171 in 1195, 0.67 dB) is structurally exact; multi-tone / LTE PUSCH use an M-sample DMRS block per 7 symbols (not modelled)
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: Qualcomm FSM: 256-point FFT over the DMRS phases per 32 ms (single-tone) block + quadratic interpolation, search +-250 Hz, not iterative, no decoder step [paper]; CFO mostly removed by the UE locking to the downlink
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: UE +-0.1 / 0.2 ppm vs the DL [std] = 0.013 at 900 MHz, 15 kHz [derived]; DMRS range +-1 kHz = +-0.071, deployed search +-250 Hz = +-0.018 [paper, derived]; NTN without GNSS ~0.043 [derived]; ours 0.03 with the full +-0.071 window
  - Published sync loss: not reported vs ideal sync; NPUSCH F1 1 tone 1 rep: 3GPP -2.1 dB, Qualcomm sim -4.5, bench -4.2 (70 % throughput); fixed point < 0.5 dB [paper]
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): no AWGN MCS thresholds in 3GPP; low SNR by repetition (up to 128) + HARQ at 10 % BLER; LTE CQI 1-6 (QPSK, code rate 78-602 / 1024) at ~-7 ... +3 dB, 10 % BLER, OFDM (indicative) [std, paper]: not comparable
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the deployed one because its window is the full DMRS range +-0.071 instead of the deployed +-0.018 and R-B uses all 171 pilots coherently: an upper bound for DMRS-style receivers
  - Sources: [Chakrapani 2019](https://arxiv.org/abs/1906.07096), [NB-IoT primer](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1606.04171); lit_card_terr.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.7

### Sparse pilots 1/32

- Real system / standard: C11 generic sparse pilots 1/32 (400ZR-inspired; 400ZR cited for its +-0.03 CFO requirement only)
- Link: https://www.ieee802.org/3/ct/public/19_05/zhang_3ct_01a_0519.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `zr32`, K = 1057: ([k 1] [d 31]) x 33 [d 1]
- Nk = 33; pilot energy 0.14 dB; airtime +3.2 %
- Estimator chain: 4th-power coarse, Rife-Boorstyn on the pilots
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over the +-1/32 alias lobes (part files: window 4-8, lobes 1, n_hyp 26-50)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: 400ZR: +-1.8 GHz at 59.8 GBd = 0.030 (= ours)
- What we changed vs the real system: not 400ZR (dual-pol 16QAM optics, continuous carrier recovery, Es/N0 ~19 dB; its coarse stage uses training sequences per Wang 2025, unverified against OIF); only the pilot density
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: OIF-400ZR IA 02.0 (2020) / 03.0 (2024) (PDF not parseable, not read) [std]; IEEE 802.3ct contribution (Zhang 2019) [std]
  - Real deployments: 400ZR DCI coherent pluggables (DP-16QAM, 59.84375 GBd), proprietary vendor DSPs [vendor]; 800G LR moved the pilot interval 32 -> 64 [paper]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: 1 QPSK pilot / 32 symbols (3.1 %) + an 11-symbol training sequence per 3712-symbol sub-frame (0.3 %) [std, derived]; ours: the 1/32 grid (33 pilots, 0.14 dB), no training sequence
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: continuous: training sequences for frame sync and initial frequency-offset locking, pilots for carrier phase (Wang 2025, unverified against the OIF IA) [paper]; fallback FFT-FOE + blind phase search; no decoder step
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: Rx tolerance +-1.8 GHz at 59.84 GBd = 0.030 (datasheets +-3.6 GHz = 0.060) [std, vendor, derived]; = ours
  - Published sync loss: not found (proprietary)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): none in -5 ... +3 dB (DP-16QAM CFEC threshold Es/N0 ~19 dB) [derived]
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: WEAKER in observation length (one 1024-symbol burst, no training sequence, vs continuous averaging over millions of symbols) but STRONGER in its decoder-checked lobe search; cited only for the +-0.03 requirement
  - Sources: [Zhang 2019](https://www.ieee802.org/3/ct/public/19_05/zhang_3ct_01a_0519.pdf), [Wang 2025](https://www.researching.cn/ArticlePdf/m00005/2025/23/6/060602.pdf); lit_card_terr.md 4

### WiFi pre128

- Real system / standard: C12 IEEE 802.11-inspired coarse / fine preamble estimation (legacy L-STF / L-LTF)
- Link: https://www.mathworks.com/help/wlan/ref/wlancoarsecfoestimate.html
- Known-symbol layout: `wifi128`, K = 1152: [k 128] [d 1024]
- Nk = 128; pilot energy 0.51 dB; airtime +12.5 %
- Estimator chain: lag-16 autocorrelation coarse, lag-64 fine on an 8 x 16 repeated block
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over the +-4 fine lobes (1/64; the lag-16 range 1/32 wraps) (part files: window 18-62, lobes 4, n_hyp 332-1124)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: 802.11: +-20 ppm -> coarse range +-1/32 = 0.031 at lag 16 (lit_real_wlan.md)
- What we changed vs the real system: OFDM STF / LTF mapped to a single-carrier 8 x 16 repeated block
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: IEEE 802.11-2020 (OFDM PHY cl. 17; the legacy preamble also in HT / VHT / HE) [std, not read; via MathWorks / Keysight]
  - Real deployments: every Wi-Fi chip runs the legacy L-STF / L-LTF preamble [vendor]; OpenOFDM open FPGA receiver [vendor]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: L-STF 160 samples (10 x 16) + L-LTF 160 (32 GI + 2 x 64) = 320 known at 20 MS/s: 1.18 dB if charged like ours [std, derived]; ours 8 x 16 = 128 (0.51 dB), the lag-64 stage on the same 16-periodic block (the real LTF is a separate 64-periodic sequence)
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: lag-16 L-STF autocorrelation coarse (range +-0.031), lag-64 L-LTF fine (+-0.0078); the coarse picks the fine lobe, no retry; pilot subcarriers track phase [vendor]; OpenOFDM runs only the lag-16 coarse stage [vendor]; no decoder step
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: +-20 ppm per side -> +-40 ppm relative = 0.0117 cycles/sample at 5.8 GHz, 0.0062 at 2.4 GHz [derived]; ours 0.03 (2.6-4.8x larger)
  - Published sync loss: not found (no readable STF / LTF RMSE or implementation-loss source)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): none in -5 ... +3 dB: 6 Mb/s (BPSK 1/2) sensitivity -82 dBm at PER 10 % ~ +4 dB SNR [std, derived]; 802.11ad SC (different structure, not modelled) MCS 2-4 at ~0.5-2.5 dB, SE 0.44-0.66 = 0.56-0.67x ours [std via Wikipedia, derived]
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: fewer known samples (128 vs 320) and no phase tracking (weaker), but 0.51 instead of 1.18 dB energy and a CRC search over +-4 fine lobes the real chip does not have (stronger)
  - Sources: [wlanCoarseCFOEstimate](https://www.mathworks.com/help/wlan/ref/wlancoarsecfoestimate.html), [OpenOFDM](https://openofdm.readthedocs.io/en/latest/freq_offset.html); lit_card_terr.md 2, lit_rates.md 4.6

### MIL-110 HF

- Real system / standard: C13 MIL-STD-188-110C App. C serial-tone HF modem
- Link: https://www.sigidwiki.com/images/c/c8/MIL-STD-188_110C_CHG_NOTICE-1.pdf
- Known-symbol layout: `mil110`, K = 1332: [k 184] ([d 256] [k 31]) x 4
- Nk = 308; pilot energy 1.14 dB; airtime +30.1 %
- Estimator chain: Rife-Boorstyn over preamble + probes (all 308 known symbols), constant phase
- CRC / lobe search: CRC search over the +-3 probe-period lobes (1/287) (part files: window 1-2, lobes 3, n_hyp 20-34)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: requirement +-75 Hz at 2400 Bd = 0.031 (= ours)
- What we changed vs the real system: 184 preamble + 4 x (256 d + 31 probe) scaled to 1024 data (drops the 103-symbol rate / interleaver section: real 411 known, 1.47 dB); the real modem has an adaptive equaliser and interleaver frames
- Data: e5-10, e5-15, e5-2, e5-25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: MIL-STD-188-110C with CN1, App C (= STANAG 4539, ITU-R F.763-5 Annex 6); superseded by MIL-STD-188-110D (29 Dec 2017) [std]
  - Real deployments: L3Harris Falcon III RF-7800H-MP (110C App D in 2019, 110D in 2022; 50 000 Falcon III units by 2023) [vendor]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: App C: 287-symbol preamble (184 sync / Doppler + 103 rate / interleaver incl. a 31-symbol mini-probe), then 256 data + 31-symbol mini-probe frames, +72 known every 72 frames: 411 known per 1024 data (1.47 dB) [std, derived]; ours 184 + 4 x 31 = 308 (1.14 dB) drops the 103-symbol section
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: preamble detection / Doppler removal, then probe- and equaliser-driven tracking (MathWorks 110A: cross-spectrum phase fit + PI loop + NCO, RLS DFE) [vendor]; no one-shot estimator published for App C / D; no decoder step
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: +-75 Hz at 2400 Bd = 0.031 (= ours), tested at 30 dB SNR; Doppler rate 3.5 Hz/s; low-SNR acquisition 'not yet standardized' [std]
  - Published sync loss: not found
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): none in -5 ... +3 dB with an AWGN requirement: 2400 bps serial 8PSK 1/2 Es/N0 ~11 dB; App C 3200 bps AWGN 9 dB SNR in 3 kHz (~10 dB Es/N0); App D WID 1-4 BPSK 1/8-2/3, SE 0.06-0.5 [std]
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the real App C because it charges ~0.33 dB less energy and adds an all-symbol R-B estimate and a CRC lobe search at SNRs the real modem does not specify (AWGN only)
  - Sources: [MIL-STD-188-110C](https://www.sigidwiki.com/images/c/c8/MIL-STD-188_110C_CHG_NOTICE-1.pdf), [F.763-5](https://www.itu.int/dms_pubrec/itu-r/rec/f/R-REC-F.763-5-200501-P!!PDF-E.pdf); lit_card_terr.md 3, lit_rates.md 4.8

### hyb3

- Real system / standard: C14 Advantech satellite modems, hybrid DA + NDA (patent US 7590199)
- Link: https://patents.google.com/patent/US7590199
- Known-symbol layout: `prepost16`, K = 1056: [k 16] [d 1024] [k 16]
- Nk = 32; pilot energy 0.13 dB; airtime +3.1 %
- Estimator chain: log DA spectrum (16 + 16 known) + log 4th-power spectrum summed, 3 best peaks
- CRC / lobe search: metric-ordered CRC search over the 3 candidates + their lobes (part files: window 2-5, lobes 3, n_hyp 34-76)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: patent: +-3 % of Rs = 0.03 (= ours)
- What we changed vs the real system: 16 + 16 pre / post instead of the patent's 48-symbol contiguous preamble + 440 payload (US 7590199); candidates chosen by CRC + rho instead of mean |LLR| after partial turbo decoding (US 7593490)
- Data: e5-pp10, e5-pp15, e5-pp2, e5-pp25, e5-smoke-a, e5-smoke-b; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: patents US 7590199 B2 (2009, hybrid estimator) + US 7593490 B2 (2009, joint synchroniser / decoder), Advantech; Alagha-Erup SPSC 2003 not found [patent]
  - Real deployments: DVB-RCS gen-1 MF-TDMA return link; Advantech DVB-RCS / S2 hubs and terminals to the US DoD (2010) [vendor]; whether products run the patented estimator: not found; ESA joint turbo decoding + carrier sync project (FPGA) [agency]
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: 48-symbol contiguous preamble + 440 payload = 488 symbols, QPSK, rate-1/2 turbo (9.8 % known) [patent]; ours 16 + 16 pre / post (0.13 dB): a 1/1040 fringe comb instead of one ~1/48 DA lobe that gates the 4th-power peaks
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: DA (r c*) and NDA (phase x 4) log spectra summed with weights, 3 candidates by peak removal + parabolic interpolation (7590199); per candidate: derotation, decision-aided phase tracking, partial turbo decoding (1-3 of ~10 iterations), pick the largest mean |LLR| (7593490) [patent]
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: Ka 1e-8 RMS x 6 = +-1800 Hz at 64 kBd = +-0.028 [patent, derived]; = ours
  - Published sync loss: no numbers published [patent]
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): rate-1/2 turbo QPSK, threshold not published; DVB-RCS / RCS2 waveforms are the proxy (see RCS2-13)
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: the candidate choice (CRC + rho over candidates and lobes) is STRONGER than mean |LLR|, but the 16 + 16 layout is WEAKER than the patent's 48-symbol preamble (no gating lobe) and the phase is constant instead of decision-aided tracking
  - Sources: [US 7590199](https://patents.google.com/patent/US7590199), [US 7593490](https://patents.google.com/patent/US7593490); lit_card_sat.md 4

### G4096

- Real system / standard: C15 DVB-RCS2 ID 13, Griebel & Wasenmueller & Wehn, Adv. Radio Sci. 19, 59 (2021) (REFERENCES C4)
- Link: https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/
- Known-symbol layout: `rcs13`, K = 1140: [k 32] ([d 19] [k 1]) x 53 [d 17] [k 31]
- Nk = 116; pilot energy 0.47 dB; airtime +11.3 %
- Estimator chain: the paper's DA-KS: zero-filled r c*, FFT 4096 + 3-bin parabolic interpolation
- CRC / lobe search: as RCS2-13 (part files: window 2-3, lobes 0, n_hyp 4-6)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: paper: |f| <= 1.5 % of Rs = 0.015 (half of ours); TR 101 545-4 range 0.5-3 %
- What we changed vs the real system: FPGA fixed-point word lengths not modelled; FFT 4096 = the paper's ~3.8x zero-padding for our ~1140-symbol burst
- Data: ars-10, ars-15, ars-2, ars-25; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ETSI EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1 (2026-04, current; NGSO, roll-off 0.20 / 0.10 / 0.05, S2X waveforms on the return link) [std]; Annex A ID 13 unchanged from V1.3.1 (used in est4) [std]; guidelines TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1 (2026) [std]
  - Real deployments: MF-TDMA return link (terminal -> hub); DVB-RCS gen 1: > 370 networks, > 180 000 terminals (2010) [vendor]; RCS2 in STM SatLink (2012), Gilat SkyEdge, Creonic hub IP [vendor]; a current RCS2 terminal count: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: ID 13: 1616 symbols, QPSK 1/3, pre 32 + post 31 + 77 one-symbol pilots (P = 20) = 140 known (8.7 %, 0.39 dB) [std, derived]; ours: same pre / post / P scaled to 1024 data, 116 known (0.47 dB): equivalent
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: no TC-LM estimator in the standard or TR (TR: frequency from the preamble) [std]; the one documented implementation (Griebel 2021): DA-KS R-B FFT over all known symbols + 3-bin interpolation, constant phase, one decode [paper]; one shot per burst, the NCC closes a slow per-terminal frequency loop (10 Hz steps) [std]; no phase tracking in any RCS2 source
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: TR 101 545-4: 0.005-0.03 (0.5-3 % of Rs) [std]; Griebel simulates |f| <= 0.015 [paper]; ours 0.03 = the top of the range
  - Published sync loss: Griebel best DA-KS 0.12-0.15 dB vs genie at FER 1e-3 [paper]; TR bench, all impairments: ~0.5 dB (QPSK 1/3), ~0.6 dB (QPSK 1/2) at PER 1e-5 [std]; ours ~0 dB except 1/10 (0.63 dB, 1/20 alias)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, ideal-sync AWGN, PER 1e-3 (TR 101 545-4 10.2.1) [std], net SE [derived]: ID 42 BPSK 1/3 (3236 sym) -3.81 dB 0.304; ID 43 BPSK 1/2 -1.53 dB 0.465; ID 13 QPSK 1/3 (1616) -0.80 dB 0.609; ID 3 QPSK 1/3 (536) -0.27 dB 0.567; ID 2 (262) 0.50 dB 0.427; ID 14 QPSK 1/2 (1616) 1.49 dB 0.931; ID 4 QPSK 1/2 (536) 1.92 dB 0.881; at the same Es/N0 0.56-1.13x our SE (ID 42 0.95x with the bench loss)
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the real system because it gets a metric-ordered CRC search, an 8192-point FFT, the exact LS phase and genie timing; the deployed chain would be 0.1-0.5 dB worse
  - Sources: [EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf), [TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/101500_101599/10154504/01.02.01_60/tr_10154504v010201p.pdf), [Griebel 2021](https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/); lit_card_sat.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.3

  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: TR 101 545-4: 0.005-0.03 (0.5-3 % of Rs) [std]; Griebel simulates |f| <= 0.015 [paper]; ours 0.03 = the top of the range
  - Published sync loss: Griebel best DA-KS 0.12-0.15 dB vs genie at FER 1e-3 [paper]; TR bench, all impairments: ~0.5 dB (QPSK 1/3), ~0.6 dB (QPSK 1/2) at PER 1e-5 [std]; ours ~0 dB except 1/10 (0.63 dB, 1/20 alias)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, ideal-sync AWGN, PER 1e-3 (TR 101 545-4 10.2.1) [std], net SE [derived]: ID 42 BPSK 1/3 (3236 sym) -3.81 dB 0.304; ID 43 BPSK 1/2 -1.53 dB 0.465; ID 13 QPSK 1/3 (1616) -0.80 dB 0.609; ID 3 QPSK 1/3 (536) -0.27 dB 0.567; ID 2 (262) 0.50 dB 0.427; ID 14 QPSK 1/2 (1616) 1.49 dB 0.931; ID 4 QPSK 1/2 (536) 1.92 dB 0.881; at the same Es/N0 0.56-1.13x our SE (ID 42 0.95x with the bench loss)
  - Verdict: our version is MIXED vs the real system: the paper's one-decode chain (faithful) with genie timing (stronger) and |f| <= 0.03 instead of the paper's 0.015 (harder)
  - Sources: [EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf), [TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/101500_101599/10154504/01.02.01_60/tr_10154504v010201p.pdf), [Griebel 2021](https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/); lit_card_sat.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.3

### RCS2-13 lobes

- Real system / standard: C15 / RCS2-13 + the +-1/20 alias lobes in the CRC search (our addition, not from the paper)
- Link: expected_ars.md
- Known-symbol layout: `rcs13`, K = 1140: [k 32] ([d 19] [k 1]) x 53 [d 17] [k 31]
- Nk = 116; pilot energy 0.47 dB; airtime +11.3 %
- Estimator chain: as RCS2-13
- CRC / lobe search: as RCS2-13 + 1 alias lobe of 1/20 each side (part files: window 2-3, lobes 1, n_hyp 14-20)
- Design CFO range of the real system vs our +-0.03: as RCS2-13
- What we changed vs the real system: lobe search added by us
- Data: ars-10, ars-15, ars-2, ars-25; label in plot_e4 STY / STY5 / STYA: yes
- Real system (lit_card_sat / space / terr.md, lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15):
  - Current standard / source: ETSI EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1 (2026-04, current; NGSO, roll-off 0.20 / 0.10 / 0.05, S2X waveforms on the return link) [std]; Annex A ID 13 unchanged from V1.3.1 (used in est4) [std]; guidelines TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1 (2026) [std]
  - Real deployments: MF-TDMA return link (terminal -> hub); DVB-RCS gen 1: > 370 networks, > 180 000 terminals (2010) [vendor]; RCS2 in STM SatLink (2012), Gilat SkyEdge, Creonic hub IP [vendor]; a current RCS2 terminal count: not found
  - Real known-symbol layout vs ours: ID 13: 1616 symbols, QPSK 1/3, pre 32 + post 31 + 77 one-symbol pilots (P = 20) = 140 known (8.7 %, 0.39 dB) [std, derived]; ours: same pre / post / P scaled to 1024 data, 116 known (0.47 dB): equivalent
  - Real estimator, acquisition / tracking: no TC-LM estimator in the standard or TR (TR: frequency from the preamble) [std]; the one documented implementation (Griebel 2021): DA-KS R-B FFT over all known symbols + 3-bin interpolation, constant phase, one decode [paper]; one shot per burst, the NCC closes a slow per-terminal frequency loop (10 Hz steps) [std]; no phase tracking in any RCS2 source
  - Design CFO range [cycles/symbol]: TR 101 545-4: 0.005-0.03 (0.5-3 % of Rs) [std]; Griebel simulates |f| <= 0.015 [paper]; ours 0.03 = the top of the range
  - Published sync loss: Griebel best DA-KS 0.12-0.15 dB vs genie at FER 1e-3 [paper]; TR bench, all impairments: ~0.5 dB (QPSK 1/3), ~0.6 dB (QPSK 1/2) at PER 1e-5 [std]; ours ~0 dB except 1/10 (0.63 dB, 1/20 alias)
  - Code rates / MODCODs in -5 ... +3 dB (required Es/N0, net SE): turbo, ideal-sync AWGN, PER 1e-3 (TR 101 545-4 10.2.1) [std], net SE [derived]: ID 42 BPSK 1/3 (3236 sym) -3.81 dB 0.304; ID 43 BPSK 1/2 -1.53 dB 0.465; ID 13 QPSK 1/3 (1616) -0.80 dB 0.609; ID 3 QPSK 1/3 (536) -0.27 dB 0.567; ID 2 (262) 0.50 dB 0.427; ID 14 QPSK 1/2 (1616) 1.49 dB 0.931; ID 4 QPSK 1/2 (536) 1.92 dB 0.881; at the same Es/N0 0.56-1.13x our SE (ID 42 0.95x with the bench loss)
  - Verdict: our version is STRONGER than the real system: as RCS2-13, plus a +-1/20 alias-lobe search no RCS2 source has
  - Sources: [EN 301 545-2 V1.5.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/301500_301599/30154502/01.05.01_60/en_30154502v010501p.pdf), [TR 101 545-4 V1.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/101500_101599/10154504/01.02.01_60/tr_10154504v010201p.pdf), [Griebel 2021](https://ars.copernicus.org/articles/19/59/2021/); lit_card_sat.md 1, lit_rates.md 4.3

## C. Summary per target

Required SNR on the plotted axis (pilot energy charged), points >= -5 dB only; CI = plot_final.req (Clopper-Pearson bound curves of the bracketing points; '-' = that bound does not cross). Gap = receiver - blind (+ = blind better). Raw axis = the same crossing vs the data symbols' Es/N0 (pilot energy NOT charged; secondary). MACs per core = scheduled count at the required SNR (plot_final.core_at). Ratio = blind / receiver MACs per core. Bracket = the two points the interpolation uses (errors / packets). Predictions: written before the runs; '-' = no numeric prediction.

## D. Fairness and caveats (from competitors.md, 'Assumptions' and 'Is the comparison fair?')

- "Timing and burst detection are assumed known for every receiver, blind included (out of scope for now, user 2026-11-15). In a real system the preamble also does detection and timing, so this assumption favours blind: the comparison is CFO/phase + decoding only."
- "Same FEC for all (our polar N = 2048 + CRC-16, CA-SCL-8); real systems use turbo / LDPC / BCH." Same re-encoding check (RHO_N) and metric-ordered CRC search (incl. alias / lobe hypotheses) for all.
- "Blind is NOT packet-paired with the est4 competitors (different harness and seeds); est4 competitors are paired with each other. Differences below ~0.1 dB between blind and a competitor are inside this unpaired noise." Seeds in the included parts: ars-10 [52], ars-15 [51], ars-2 [54], ars-25 [53], e4-10 [52], e4-15 [51], e4-2 [54], e4-25 [53], e4-pp10 [52], e4-pp15 [51], e4-pp2 [54], e4-pp25 [53], e5-10 [62], e5-10 + e5-10lo-a [], e5-10 + e5-10lo-b [], e5-10 + e5-10lo-c [], e5-15 [63], e5-2 [65], e5-25 [64], e5-pp10 [62], e5-pp15 [63], e5-pp2 [65], e5-pp25 [64]; the ARS variants are paired with RCS2-13 (same seeds and bursts); est5 jobs use seeds 62-65, so est5 will not be packet-paired with est4 either.
- "Complexity is modelled (MACs per core, scheduled count), not measured on hardware."
- "CFO uniform in +-0.03 cycles/symbol for everyone. Several real systems are designed for a smaller range (DVB-S2 header acquisition, CCSDS, Wi-Fi fine stage); we widen them with their own lobe / alias CRC search rather than reject them. Static CFO."
- "Pilots are OUTSIDE the packet ... Pilot energy is charged" (SNR = Es/N0 + 10 log10((1024 + Nk) / 1024)); the airtime cost (Nk / 1024) is reported, not charged. The raw-axis required SNR in section C is the secondary view where the pilot energy is NOT charged.
- Blind = the testing model (plot_e4.blind): 1/10, 0.15 = run.py testing_s11g, 64k packets per SNR; 2/5 = testing at 0.5, 1.0 dB only; 1/2 = testing at 2.0, 2.5 dB, and the **1/2 1.5 dB point is still the presentation receiver (mixed model)** until the running tw25 job lands. The other points without MACs are presentation-receiver points too.
- tw parts (testing model over the full range, expected_tw.md): landed: blind_ber_t-tw10a2.json, blind_ber_t-tw10b.json, blind_ber_t-tw15a2.json, blind_ber_t-tw15b.json, blind_ber_t-tw2.json, blind_ber_t-tw25.json, blind_ber_t-twf2.json, blind_ber_t-twf25.json. plot_e4.blind (agg_cmp.adopted / qfix) pools every landed tw part (agg_cmp TW_PARTS / TW_QFX) per SNR, so they enter sections B-D here as soon as they land.
- Genie at 1/10: 8.2a's -5.19 dB interpolates with its -5.5 dB point; under the -5 dB rule it is only '< -5.0' here. RCS2-13 at 1/10 -5.03 dB plotted (raw -5.5, 881 / 480k) and the RCS2 pre/post -5.37 dB point are likewise not shown.

## E. Spectral efficiency vs the real systems (lit_rates.md, 2026-11-15)

Net SE = information bits per transmitted symbol, header / preamble / pilots / syncwords included; ours = (K - 16) / 1024 (CRC-16 not counted, no other overhead). Competitor thresholds are mostly ideal-sync AWGN table values at their own targets (S2 PER 1e-7, S2X FER 1e-5, RCS2 PER 1e-3, CCSDS BER 1e-6 ~ FER 1e-4, VDE-SAT unstated); ours are measured with blind CFO / phase recovery over +-0.03 (8.2a). Real implementation losses would move theirs 0.1-0.6 dB right. Per-system MODCOD lists: the Real system blocks of section A; full table: lit_rates.md section 3.

### Best real SE at our operating points (lit_rates.md section 6)

| Our point (rate, Es/N0, net SE) | Best competitor SE at the same Es/N0 (x = ratio to ours) | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| 1/10, -4.9 dB, 0.185 | CCSDS turbo 1/6, k 8920: 0.332 (x1.78). S2X BPSK 4/15 short: <= 0.244 (x1.32). S2X BPSK 11/45: 0.230 at 0.6 dB lower. VDE-SAT 0.50-2: 0.19 at +0.4 dB (x0.88). No DVB-RCS2 mode. | Behind the long-block standards; only part is closable at 1024 symbols (the bound allows 0.300); the largest code gap of our four rates |
| 0.15, -3.6 dB, 0.284 | VDE-SAT 0.50-3: 0.325 (x1.14). RCS2 ID 42: 0.304 (x1.13 ideal, x0.95 with the bench loss). S2X BPSK 1/3 medium: 0.317 at -4.0 dB (x1.25). | Slightly behind on paper (10-25 %); about a tie with the short-burst ID 42 once its real loss is counted |
| (gap) -2.9 ... -1 dB | CCSDS turbo 1/4 (0.498 at -2.86), VDE-SAT 1.50-1 (0.475 at -2.4), S2 QPSK 1/4 (0.48 at -2.35), S2X 13/45 (0.55 at -2.03) | We have no rate here (a rate ~1/4, SE ~0.48, would fill it; not tested) |
| 2/5, 0.61 dB, 0.784 | S2X QPSK 7/15 short: 0.874 (x1.12). S2 QPSK 2/5 normal: 0.77-0.79 at 0.9 dB lower. RCS2 ID 13 / VDE-SAT 1.50-2: ~0.6 (x0.8 at 0.61). | Behind S2X by ~12 % at 16 200-bit frames; ahead of the short-burst standards |
| 1/2, 1.79 dB, 0.984 | S2X QPSK 11/20 normal: 1.06 at 1.45 dB; 8/15 short: 1.00 at 1.45 dB; S2 QPSK 1/2: 0.97 at 1.00 dB; CCSDS turbo 1/2: 1.00 at 1.10 dB; RCS2 ID 14: 0.931 at 1.49 dB; CCSDS LDPC (2048, 1024): 0.985 at 2.20 dB @ 1e-5; Comtech QPSK turbo 1/2: 0.982 at 1.72 dB guaranteed | Equal SE; the long-block standards need 0.3-0.8 dB less Es/N0; the same-length LDPC (2048, 1024) is equal or behind |

Pilot / header overhead of these standards is 2-10 % SE (S2 normal 2.4 %, S2X short 2-3 %, VL-SNR 7.5-10 %, RCS2 ID 13 / 14 7-9 %, VDE-SAT 2-5 %); the +5-20 % SE differences come mainly from block length (4-60x ours) and code / decoder quality.

### Finite-length bound for a 1024-symbol packet (recomputed, lit_rates.md last section)

Normal approximation (Polyanskiy-Poor-Verdu) for QPSK = 2 x BI-AWGN, n = 2048 real channel uses, log2 M ~ nC - sqrt(nV) Q^-1(eps) + 0.5 log2 n, C and V by numerical integration (QPSK capacity at 0 dB = 0.972 bits/symbol). Replaces the hand estimate of lit_rates.md section 2.

| Our point | Es/N0 [dB] (blind, 8.2a) | BLER | QPSK capacity [bits/sym] | max SE of any 1024-symbol packet | ours (net SE) | ours / max |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1/10 | -4.90 | 1e-4 | 0.403 | 0.300 | 0.185 | 62 % |
| 0.15 | -3.60 | 1e-3 | 0.520 | 0.427 | 0.284 | 67 % |
| 2/5 | 0.61 | 1e-3 | 1.065 | 0.959 | 0.784 | 82 % |
| 1/2 | 1.79 | 1e-3 | 1.251 | 1.149 | 0.984 | 86 % |

0.15 @ 1e-4 (-2.98 dB) is not in the bound table.
